@\+Summary Default Interrupt Handler for IOCAF1 pin

@\+Returns None.

@\+Param None.

@\+Example \doxylink{pin__manager_8c_a50357774183a136d9490f64ad0d5c6cb}{PIN\+\_\+\+MANAGER\+\_\+\+Initialize()}; IOCAF1\+\_\+\+Set\+Interrupt\+Handler(\+IOCAF1\+\_\+\+Default\+Interrupt\+Handler);

void(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} IOCAF1\+\_\+\+Interrupt\+Handler) (void) (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{void}]{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [extern]}}

@\+Summary Dynamic Interrupt Handler for IOCAF1 pin

@\+Description This is a dynamic interrupt handler to be used together with the \doxylink{pin__manager_8c_a09d5a0d509182455167ed15159102378}{IOCAF1\+\_\+\+Set\+Interrupt\+Handler()} method. This handler is called every time the IOCAF1 ISR is executed and allows any function to be registered at runtime.

@\+Preconditions Pin Manager intializer called

@\+Returns None.

@\+Param None.

@\+Example \doxylink{pin__manager_8c_a50357774183a136d9490f64ad0d5c6cb}{PIN\+\_\+\+MANAGER\+\_\+\+Initialize()}; IOCAF1\+\_\+\+Set\+Interrupt\+Handler(\+IOCAF1\+\_\+\+Interrupt\+Handler);
